**September 28, 2026 S11 command-bar latency and live preview checkpoint.** `feature:commandBar` now advertises Chromium's direct WebUI autocomplete for pinned source `970adc61`; patch 0112 fixes the native capability inventory and patch 0113 returns an ordered local History/Bookmark/typed result as soon as Chromium publishes it. The shell starts the async query in a layout effect. Serial AuxDev builds `/tmp/aux-s11-command-bar-capability-build.log`, `/tmp/aux-s11-command-bar-first-local-build.log`, and `/tmp/aux-s11-command-bar-layout-effect-build.log` passed; shell manifest `101c12bb` and runtime staging passed. A disposable 50,000-URL Chromium History fixture with 20 measured queries in [`native-command-bar.mjs`](../tests/benchmarks/native-command-bar.mjs) produced p50 **30.5 ms**, p95 **30.8 ms** (bootstrap p95 interval 30.6–32.4 ms) in the [built native shell](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-command-latency-layout-2026-09-28/manifest.json), down from 214 ms p95 with the old 180 ms quiet timer. The timer-only intermediate was 80.6 ms p95. This is a battery-powered AuxDev component-build trend using CDP text insertion and the second animation frame as a conservative paint proxy; it does **not** meet or qualify the ≤30 ms M4 target. Focused command-bar tests passed 19/19; typecheck, lint, format, budgets, generated bindings, native patch validation, and diff checks passed. Separately, disposable [Reader keyboard Peek](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-reader-peek-2026-09-28/manifest.json) and [keyboard-focused sidebar tab preview](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-tab-preview-focus-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journeys passed. Continue M4 measurement on AC with a presentation timestamp and the remaining S11 interaction qualifications before advancing roadmap dependencies.

**September 28, 2026 S11 advanced Space theme checkpoint.** The Space editor now offers an accessible hue/lightness color pad, precise color dial, lightness and intensity sliders, invert lightness, seven presets plus Custom, a grain cycle, and Reset. A bounded OKLCH recipe persists through the runtime Space record, Profile organization layout, cross-window placement and shell chrome; Reset clears the custom recipe and grain. Chromium's battery-saver active signal now rides the tab catalog snapshot and change patch, suppressing the grain layer on transitions. Patches 0110–0111, generated host bindings `4770ce29`, shell manifest `624e25d5`, and serial builds `/tmp/aux-s11-battery-grain-build.log`, `/tmp/aux-s11-advanced-final-build.log`, and `/tmp/aux-s11-advanced-organization-build.log` passed with runtime staged. Focused Space/theme/catalog tests passed 34/34; typecheck, lint, format, bundle budgets, bindings, patch validation and diff checks passed. The disposable [`--space-theme-advanced`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-advanced-theme-complete-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey passed custom create/reopen/reset/reopen, including native Profile organization acceptance. The first diagnostic run exposed the native organization validator gap; its corrected patch passed. An active battery-saver transition and rendered grain/tint pixels were not inspected live. Continue the remaining S11 responsiveness and craft dependencies, then S12 and later roadmap stages.

**September 28, 2026 S11 page tint and grain checkpoint.** Chromium's tab catalog now carries the page's `theme-color` through the strict tab/page contracts to the shell; the toolbar blends the active page color into its glass and clears the tint when the page has none. Space grain now uses a deterministic pre-rendered 256 px tile with `soft-light` blending and the proposed light/dark opacity scales, replacing live SVG turbulence. The existing Space editor saves preset color and grain through the native Space API. Focused catalog tests passed 8/8, typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, bundle budgets, patch validation and diff checks passed. Patch 0109, shell manifest `420ce5cb`, and serial `/tmp/aux-s11-theme-build.log` passed with runtime staged. The disposable [`--space-theme`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-space-theme-controls-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey verified native `#a33450` projection and a teal Space with 70% grain after reopening its editor. The later checkpoint above completes the battery-saver signal and advanced 07-B controls.

**September 28, 2026 S11 link Peek checkpoint.** Reader links can open a temporary native preview with Option-click, and Library bookmarks have a Preview control. Peek keeps the source tab selected, hides its temporary page from the tab strip, and offers Open as tab or Close preview. Promotion activates the same native page ID; dismissal closes it without adding a Recently Closed entry. The shell records pending previews for crash cleanup and accepts only credential-free HTTP(S) links. `feature:peek` is qualified for pinned source `970adc61`. Focused Peek and Library tests passed 5/5; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, bundle budgets, patch validation and diff checks passed. Shell manifest `38c48c72`; the final serial `/tmp/aux-s11-link-peek-id-build.log` passed 21 steps and the runtime was staged. The disposable [`--link-peek`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-link-peek-complete-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey passed native preview, same-page promotion, and dismissal. Earlier diagnostic attempts exposed a Library overlay, invalid native page ID, and a harness assertion error; none count as acceptance evidence. Restart recovery and Reader Option-click have focused source coverage but no live interaction. Next S11 dependency is Space themes, grain and toolbar top-band tint.

**September 28, 2026 S11 split-view checkpoint.** A paired split now persists for either selected page, removes stale pair mappings when replaced or closed, and appears as a compact vertical-sidebar item with both titles, Swap and Close. Layout parsing again round-trips archived-tab metadata. The existing native SurfaceEmbeds display both pages and the keyboard/drag separator. Focused workspace and hover-card tests passed 22/22; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, bundle budgets, patch validation and diff checks passed. `feature:splitView` is qualified for pinned source `970adc61` in the generated Chromium stage. Shell manifest `063955bf`; serial `/tmp/aux-s11-split-qualified-build.log` passed 13 steps after the initial 21-step build, and runtime was staged. The disposable [`--split-view`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-split-view-final-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey passed two visible native guest surfaces and the vertical sidebar item. Two preceding failed attempts exposed the missing feature qualification and missing host-binding staging; they are not counted as passes. Hover-card delay/thumbnail/dismissal passed a component test, but a native hover interaction was not observed. Next S11 dependency is link Peek with promotion to a tab.

**September 28, 2026 S11 command-bar scope checkpoint.** Search now has Open Tabs, Recently Closed, Bookmarks, History, Extensions, Sites and Work scopes with Alt+1–8 shortcuts. Recently Closed reopens the saved tab; bookmark and history choices navigate; enabled extensions open their action, with Settings fallback. Chromium autocomplete history/bookmark rows enter their matching scope; title-and-host dedup remains within each view. Escape returns to All results before closing, and the popup shows Enter/Esc keycaps plus the enabled suggestion privacy notice. Focused command-bar tests passed 23/23; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, patch validation, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `1c07c25c`; the serial `/tmp/aux-s11-command-bar-fixed-build.log` passed 21 steps and staged runtime. The disposable [`--selector-search`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-command-bar-fixed-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey found the background Red tab in the built shell while Blue was active. The first native attempt found an invalid accessible name on the new keycap container and crashed; it was removed before the passing build. The live run did not exercise closed/bookmark/history/extension actions or a current 50k-history latency measurement; focused tests cover those actions. Next S11 dependency is split view/peek and hover-card qualification.

**September 28, 2026 S11 floating sidebar qualification.** The existing vertical sidebar state machine passed 50 scripted retention/dismissal trials across edge leeway, focus, menu and drag locks, inactive-frame gating and reactivation. The toolbar shares the lock counts and passed its focused entry/exit and menu-retention tests; nine focused tests passed across the sidebar, toolbar and mode files. The disposable [`--edge-leeway`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-sidebar-edge-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey passed real native edge entry over a guest page, persistence inside the eight-pixel exit leeway, and dismissal after leaving it. The prior serial AuxDev build and stage remain `a5719650` and `/tmp/aux-s11-memory-measure-delivery-build.log`; this qualification changed no source. Command-bar craft, split/peek, and Space theme work remain for S11.

**September 28, 2026 S11 Memory saver cycle reporting checkpoint.** Native Sleep now uses Chromium's per-page private-memory footprint estimate when it discards a tab, passes that estimate to the lifecycle unit, and returns estimated saved bytes when available. The shell keeps the latest 20 automatic cycles in Profile WebUI storage, including refusals and estimate-unavailable successes; General settings shows a bounded per-cycle list and an explicitly estimated total. The current window keeps its report if storage fails, and open Settings windows refresh on local or cross-window changes. Focused scheduler and Settings tests passed 3/3; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, bundle budgets, patch validation and diff checks passed. Shell manifest `a5719650`; serial `/tmp/aux-s11-memory-measure-delivery-build.log` passed 21 steps with runtime staging. Disposable [`--memory-saver-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-memory-measure-sleep-final-2026-09-28/manifest.json) passed automatic sleep at one fixture request and lazy reload at two. The live journey did not inspect the returned estimate or Settings report; the Settings component test displayed a 12 MB estimated cycle. The other S11 deliverables remain open.

**September 28, 2026 S11 restart lazy restore checkpoint.** Ordinary background tabs now remain native session placeholders through restart; the selected tab (plus pinned/app tabs) loads at startup. Chromium's restored-tab app classification was corrected so ordinary tabs do not enter the eager-load set. The catalog and runtime carry placeholder lifecycle without attaching CDP until focus, and runtime fallback recovery opens only the selected saved tab per window. Pending tabs keep their durable IDs, titles and URLs in the shell strip; selecting one restores it before activation. Session Recovery displays actual failed restores, not intentionally deferred tabs. Focused runtime/shell tests passed 17/17; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, bundle budgets, patch validation and diff checks passed. Shell manifest `edf937e3`; serial `/tmp/aux-s11-lazy-restore-final-build.log` passed 21 steps with runtime staging. The disposable [`--memory-saver-restart`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-lazy-restore-final-rpc-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey saved a one-minute policy, slept Red, restarted Aux, confirmed Red remained a placeholder at one request, and activated its original page ID through the authenticated API to observe the second request. Native tab clicking after restart was not qualified by that journey; the shell deferred-selection path has focused unit coverage. Per-cycle memory savings measurement and the remaining S11 items are open.

**September 28, 2026 S11 automatic Memory saver checkpoint.** General settings persist an Off/1/15/30/60 minute policy; the shell schedules one eligible idle background tab at a time and delegates the final decision to native `tabSleep`, which rechecks unsaved work and media before discard. Active, split, agent-owned/controlled, loading, audible and sleeping tabs are skipped before the call. Focused scheduler and Settings control tests passed 2/2; typecheck, lint, format, bindings, patch validation, bundle budgets and diff checks passed. Shell manifest `0d1114ac`; `/tmp/aux-s11-memory-saver-native-build.log` passed 21 serial steps with runtime staging. The disposable [`--memory-saver-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-memory-saver-rpc-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey saved the one-minute policy through the authenticated settings API, waited for the shell's sleeping icon, and confirmed lazy reload by fixture requests 1 → 2. The native Settings control was covered by a component test and not clicked in that journey. Next S11 dependency is restart lazy restore and per-cycle savings measurement; broader S11 work remains.

**September 28, 2026 S11 safe tab Sleep checkpoint.** Tab menu Sleep uses the native unsaved-work probe and a guarded Chromium discard for one unchanged, owner-controlled background tab. Agent-owned, audible, pinned, unload-handler and media-capture tabs are protected; native lifecycle reaches the shell and displays a sleeping icon in the tab strip. `feature:tabLifecycle` is qualified for source `970adc61`. Focused TabMenu tests passed 2/2; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, patch validation, bundle budgets and diff checks passed. Shell manifest `d181f3b4`; `/tmp/aux-s11-lifecycle-final-build.log` passed 21 serial native steps. The disposable [`--tab-sleep-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-tab-sleep-final-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey passed menu Sleep, visible sleeping icon and lazy reload on activation (fixture requests 1 → 2). The refusal branches were not each exercised live. The following automatic Memory saver batch uses this native method; restart lazy restore and other S11 work remain.

**September 28, 2026 S11 tab Archive checkpoint.** Overview can archive selected clean, owner-controlled web tabs into a bounded Profile shared organization record, close the native Chromium tabs, and restore each URL with its saved tab ID, order and Space/folder when those destinations still exist. The Profile first stores a pending entry, then marks it archived after native close; restart reconciliation resolves an interrupted close against the live catalog. The native validator in patch 0104 and generated host bindings enforce the archive contract. The `feature:archive` qualification is recorded for source `970adc61`. Focused archive/Tidy/Overview tests passed 7/7, then the archive safety tests passed 3/3 after the close-acknowledgement repair; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, patch validation, bundle budgets and diff checks passed. Shell manifest `44b666f6`; serial `/tmp/aux-s11-archive-safety-final-build.log` passed 21 native steps and runtime staging passed. The disposable [`--archive-tabs-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s11-tab-archive-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey passed two native closes, Profile persistence across full restart, and one native Restore with one archive entry remaining. The live journey used clean loopback tabs; dirty-tab refusal relies on the previously qualified native unsaved-work check and was not repeated here. The final shell also resolves a lost native close acknowledgement from Chromium’s catalog, retains an uncertain pending entry, and restores an archived Space before reopening its tab. Those last two error/Space branches have focused code verification but no dedicated native journey.

**September 28, 2026 AI Tidy durable Undo checkpoint.** The next S11 dependency was a restart-safe, reviewable AI Tidy group mutation: a compact receipt records created folders and exact tab assignments, survives in the Profile shared organization record when Chromium gives a restored window a new session ID, and allows Undo only while those folders and assignments still match. Manual moves or edits to a created folder invalidate the shared receipt so a restart cannot recreate stale undo authority. Overview shows the control only where all affected tabs are present. The `feature:aiTidy` native qualification is recorded for source `970adc61`; a local `meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor` fixture generated the proposal without a paid gateway call. Focused Tidy tests passed 4/4, typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, patch validation, bundle budgets and diff checks passed. Shell manifest `a14f8743`; the serial `/tmp/aux-s12-tidy-guard-final-build.log` passed 21 native steps with runtime staging. The disposable [`--tidy-receipt-restart`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-tidy-receipt-final-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey passed proposal review, Apply, Profile receipt persistence, full app restart and the restored native folder and Undo control. It did not click Undo after restart, so a native reversal is not yet qualified. Earlier diagnostic journeys exposed the new-window restoration gap and are not passing evidence. The build stages generated host bindings before Chromium compilation to keep host feature qualification current.

**September 28, 2026 Task receipt history checkpoint.** Ordered Chromium history deltas now record a new exact visit against a running Task only when its URL matches one agent-controlled page with one active claim and the visit occurred after the run and claim began. The owner receipt and Markdown export show the visit and an individual or whole-run removal action; external Task clients receive metadata without URL/title and cannot remove visits. Removal requires the projected visit to remain unchanged, and patch 0102 requires Chromium HistoryService to find one exact URL/time visit with the expected title before expiry. Focused history/projection/evidence/UI/export tests passed 30/30, with four focused history tests rerun after the duplicate-visit guard; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff, patch validation, and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `47b627eb`; the serial `/tmp/aux-s12-history-native-build.log` passed 22 steps and runtime staging passed. The disposable [`--receipt-history-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-receipt-history-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey passed native HistoryService projection, external redaction/denial, and owner receipt/control display. Its Task run step was seeded after a real visit, so natural run attribution and an actual native removal remain unqualified. A removed visit cannot be recovered through Aux.


**September 28, 2026 Task receipt download checkpoint.** Chromium DownloadItem updates now carry the initiating tab URL and the Profile's selected download folder through the authenticated shell into the runtime. A download becomes run-owned only when one agent-controlled page has an active claim from a running Task; the ledger records started, completed, and interrupted outcomes. Completed files gain owner-only cleanup authority only when the file was absent at start and its bytes and file identity still match; cleanup moves the file to recoverable macOS Trash, with a durable restart marker. The Tasks receipt and Markdown export show download outcomes, while external clients receive redacted metadata and cannot clean up a file. Focused ledger/projection/evidence/UI/export tests passed; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff, patch validation and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `9cc51460`; the final serial `/tmp/aux-s12-download-final-native-build.log` passed 22 steps and runtime staging passed. The disposable [`--receipt-download-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-receipt-download-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey passed native DownloadItem projection, external redaction/denial, and the owner Tasks receipt with a recoverable cleanup control. The UI fixture's run step was seeded to verify presentation; a model-funded native download and an actual owner Trash click remain to qualify. The separate bookmark native undo still awaits action-time confirmation.


**September 28, 2026 model bookmark receipt checkpoint (live undo pending confirmation).** Code mode now exposes `page.bookmark()` on a run-claimed page after a write decision, saves through Chromium BookmarkModel, and records the absent before-state plus the exact new node identity. The Task receipt and Markdown export show the change; external clients receive metadata without the title/address or undo authority. Individual and whole-run owner undo refuse a changed bookmark. Patch 0100 makes the native remove conditional on title, creation time, folder and reading-list state in one BookmarkModel operation; the local fallback compares the same fields in one SQL delete. Focused bookmark/REPL/evidence/UI/export tests passed 47/47, with a 30/30 focused rerun after the provenance correction and 4/4 on the expanded guards; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff, bundle budgets and patch validation passed. Shell manifest `35370d52`; final serial `/tmp/aux-s12-bookmark-final-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and runtime staging passed. The disposable native [`--receipt-bookmark-undo-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-receipt-bookmark-conditional-2026-09-28/manifest.json) journey confirmed a stale conditional remove was refused and displayed the owner Undo control, then timed out waiting for the unconfirmed click. A model-funded bookmark call and successful native undo are not yet qualified.

**September 28, 2026 Task receipt consequential-action checkpoint.** Completed consequential page steps now appear in the owner receipt with their observed browser post-state, uncertain status and remote-confirmation flag. The UI and Markdown export explicitly say browser observation does not prove a remote service committed the effect, and the run-level browser undo leaves these actions alone. External Task clients do not receive the observed text; Markdown includes it only with captured text selected. Focused evidence/UI/export tests passed 23/23; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `e0dfbd73`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-page-actions-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and runtime staging passed. The disposable [`--receipt-run-undo-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-receipt-page-actions-2026-09-28/manifest.json) displayed an observed click with no remote confirmation, then reverified two live form restores and the run-owned tab close. This is a receipt of browser-observed actions, not proof of remote business effects or a remote rollback mechanism.

**September 28, 2026 Task receipt whole-run local undo checkpoint.** Completed model runs now record run-owned tab opens/closes alongside form setters. The owner Task receipt shows tab effects and offers one whole-run action that reverses eligible form edits newest-first and closes still-unused background run-owned tabs. Each restore checks the recorded page identity and resulting state; activated, navigated, taken-over or already-closed tabs are skipped with a reason. Native catalog updates arriving during an open no longer overwrite run ownership. External Task clients receive tab metadata without URLs or undo authority. Focused runtime/catalog/page/receipt/UI/export tests passed 23/23, then 27/27 on the ownership repair; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `dc73b210`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-run-undo-native-build.log` passed 21 steps, the diagnostic rebuild `/tmp/aux-s12-run-undo-diagnostic-build.log` passed, and the final runtime repair was staged into AuxDev. Disposable [`--receipt-run-undo-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-receipt-run-undo-race-fixed-2026-09-28/manifest.json) restored two live form fields and closed the run-owned tab, with three durable undo markers. Two preceding native attempts exposed a catalog registration race, then verified the repair. Group, bookmark, history and download mutation before-state and reversal remain open; remote effects remain irreversible.

**September 28, 2026 Memory captured-content checkpoint.** A local high-confidence text classifier now rejects financial account, health record, credential and identity content even when the URL/title look ordinary. The native readable-page capture path checks that text before persistence; encrypted page memory rechecks it, checks page identity again after the OS key operation, and excludes older matching records from search. Space run-note extraction and prompt summaries also omit reports or claims matching the content classifier. The tab menu explains the content check to the owner. Focused sensitivity/content-memory/pipeline tests passed 17/17; typecheck, focused lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `be9ffffb`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-memory-content-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and runtime staging passed. Memory remains preview in the native host, so no owner capture/acceptance journey was run for this batch. The classifier is a conservative lexical gate, not a complete classifier for ambiguous personal data or hostile content; full native Memory qualification remains open.

**September 28, 2026 Task receipt form undo checkpoint.** Completed model form setters now record the observed post-edit state and page URL. The Task receipt shows before/after and offers an owner-only “Undo form edit” on the original active page. The runtime serializes the restore with page commands, requires the same document epoch and URL, compares the live field to the recorded post-edit state, refuses a later edit to that control, and records the undo once. External Task clients receive audit metadata without captured field values or the undo authority. Focused executor/evidence/page/UI/export tests passed 33/33; typecheck, focused lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `c55ac430`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-receipt-undo-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and runtime staging passed. Disposable [`--receipt-undo-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-receipt-undo-visible-2026-09-28/manifest.json) restored the native fixture field from 4 to 3 and showed the durable “Undone” state. Two prior runs established that the runtime undo succeeded, then exposed harness oracles reading a hidden page / an absent observation field; the final journey checked the revealed native control. This covers form setters only, and still lacks a whole-run Undo and tab/group/bookmark/history/download before-state.

**September 28, 2026 Task receipt browser audit checkpoint.** A Task receipt now gathers model-initiated form setter steps from its run ledger and shows the prior control value, checkbox state or selection alongside the outcome and run revision. It includes failed steps as uncertain and never copies the submitted value from step inputs into this audit field. Markdown export omits captured before-state unless “Include captured text” is selected. Focused evidence/export/UI tests passed 16/16; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `0085b452`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-receipt-before-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and runtime staging passed. Disposable [`--receipt-before-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-receipt-before-fixed-2026-09-28/manifest.json) passed runtime receipt and native Tasks display for a known prior `#quantity` value. The first harness attempt was interrupted after its exact-text oracle disagreed with accessibility whitespace; CUA had visually confirmed the value, and the repaired oracle passed on a fresh disposable Profile. Broader tab/group/bookmark/history/download audit and Undo remain open.

**September 28, 2026 Memory sensitivity checkpoint.** A shared metadata gate now rejects obvious banking, health, login and account pages before native readable-page capture, with a second runtime check before encrypted storage. The native tab menu omits “Remember article” on those pages and remains unavailable in Private mode. The Space memory inbox omits completed runs tied to sensitive or owner-excluded page origins, and extraction/acceptance recheck eligibility. Focused contract/content-memory/pipeline tests passed 14/14; typecheck, focused lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `d8d601c5`; `/tmp/aux-s12-memory-sensitivity-native-build.log` passed 21 serial steps and runtime staging passed. This is a heuristic boundary, not the complete 05-M classifier: ambiguous content and intervening navigation still need provenance and classifier coverage, so Memory remains preview. The prior native preview journey remains the relevant UI evidence; this batch did not repeat it.

**September 28, 2026 Space memory history checkpoint.** Approved Space memory now keeps up to ten encrypted revision snapshots. Settings can inspect a bounded summary of each revision and restore it. Forget scrubs matching notes from every restorable snapshot before rotating the generation key, so a later restore cannot resurrect forgotten data. Prompt summaries carry at most four source citations and reserve space even when other context fills the prompt. The preview Settings state now clearly explains availability without an unusable toggle. Focused service/UI tests passed 10/10; typecheck, focused lint, format, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `ab2edf23`; `/tmp/aux-s12-memory-history-native-build.log` passed 21 serial steps, and runtime staging passed. Disposable [`--memory-preview-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-memory-preview-2026-09-28/manifest.json) passed the native preview state. Owner acceptance and restore in the native app remain gated by source sensitivity and full Memory qualification.

**September 28, 2026 Space memory review checkpoint.** 03-N now stages completed Space-run report excerpts in an encrypted notes inbox instead of writing them directly to approved memory. The owner can accept or discard each sourced note; only accepted claims enter search and the bounded prompt summary. A SQLite lease and run watermark prevent concurrent or repeated consolidation, acceptance rechecks the completed source report, and Forget removes pending or approved claims while destroying the prior encryption key. Settings → Workspaces has source selection, review, summary and approved-note Forget controls; it shows an explicit preview message while the Memory feature gate is unqualified. Focused service/UI tests passed 9/9, including restart, stale evidence, lease contention and key-retirement rollback; typecheck, lint, format, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `3ce995aa`; the serial `/tmp/aux-s12-memory-inbox-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and the runtime was staged. The disposable [`--memory-inbox-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-memory-inbox-2026-09-28/manifest.json) attempt stopped at the intentional Memory preview gate before UI actions. A temporary runtime-only test enablement confirmed the runtime stage but the native host remained preview, so it was removed and the normal runtime restaged. Native owner review remains unqualified. Next complete source sensitivity, recoverable history, and native qualification before turning Memory on.

**September 28, 2026 command Hooks checkpoint.** Tasks can now register a `run.start` or `run.end` Hook that runs an exact local command from a reviewed writable Space folder. Approval pins the handler fingerprint and verifies the current folder binding; dispatch checks the binding again and executes with the macOS seatbelt, network off, output/time caps, and an abort signal for revocation. Focused Hook/bash/UI tests passed 17/17; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `33402b73`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-hooks-command-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and runtime staging passed. Disposable [`--hooks-command-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-hooks-command-2026-09-28/manifest.json) registered `pwd` in a disposable reviewed folder, approved the exact Hook, completed a real seatbelted test call, and revoked it. Automatic event dispatch and prompt/agent handlers remain open.

**September 28, 2026 run lifecycle Hooks checkpoint.** Grant-bound MCP Hooks now support both `run.start` and `run.end`. Tasks lets the owner choose the event before exact handler approval. The runtime dispatches a queued start and terminal end with separate stable event identities; the invocation journal deduplicates repeated updates. Focused service/UI tests passed 5/5; typecheck, lint, format, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `a2eae541`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-hooks-lifecycle-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and runtime staging passed. Disposable [`--hooks-start-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-hooks-start-2026-09-28/manifest.json) registered a run-start Hook, reviewed and approved its fingerprint, called the exact MCP grant in a supervised test, and revoked it. An automatic native run-start call was not exercised. Remaining 03-M events, command/prompt/agent handlers, and trust states remain open.

**September 28, 2026 command escalation checkpoint.** 03-P's supported literal-shell path is now end to end: `bash` accepts a justified `requireEscalated` request; the runtime rejects substitutions, redirections, multiline commands and background jobs, splits up to eight literal segments, and requires a local owner decision for each before executing outside the seatbelt. Settings → Workspaces reviews the exact segment, working folder and reason, then permits one dispatch, amends a narrowly reusable `pwd`/`ls` prefix, declines, or revokes a prefix. One-time grants are consumed atomically. Focused service/bash/UI/method tests passed 28/28, followed by a real macOS disposable `pwd` approval/dispatch test 4/4. Typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `9e7702a5`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-command-escalation-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and runtime staging passed. Disposable [`--command-escalation-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-command-escalation-2026-09-28/manifest.json) showed the exact request and declined it through native Settings. The live native UI did not approve an escalated command; the focused macOS process test covers dispatch. Continue remaining 03-M Hooks, 03-N Memory and wider roadmap dependencies.

**September 28, 2026 Goal status checkpoint.** Goal runs now reserve their run identity before launch and always use the tool-call agent loop. The model can complete through verified `submit_result`, pause through `goal_update`, or report a blocker; only an identical blocker on three consecutive model turns pauses the run as blocked. The owner budget cannot be changed through the model tool. Tasks shows the blocker and refreshes active Goals, with owner resume/cancel. Focused runtime/tool/UI tests passed 19/19; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `03ae8bd1`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-goals-status-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and runtime staging passed. Disposable [`--goals-status-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-goals-status-2026-09-28/manifest.json) showed the blocker and exact 1,200-token budget in native Tasks and cancelled the fixture. A model-funded Goal turn remains unqualified. Continue 03-P Workspaces escalation and remaining roadmap dependencies.

**September 28, 2026 conversation Goals checkpoint.** A Goal created while a conversation is open now belongs to that conversation, and the runtime rejects another unfinished Goal there. The run inherits the same conversation ID and retains the owner-set token/cost/time limits; model code remains barred from Goal management. Tasks disables New Goal with a clear explanation until the current Goal finishes or is cancelled. Focused runtime/UI tests passed 5/5; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `45a2eeea`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-goals-thread-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and the runtime was staged. Disposable [`--goals-thread-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-goals-thread-2026-09-28/manifest.json) passed active-conversation binding, exact 1,200-token budget, disabled duplicate creation, cancellation and the next Goal. A live model-funded start and 03-L blocked-after-three-turn status reporting remain open.

**September 28, 2026 Workspaces checkpoint.** Settings → Workspaces now reviews a canonical local folder path, read or read/write mode, `AGENTS.md` text and an exact fingerprint before binding it to a Space. The runtime pins the reviewed context, omits changed instructions, gives file tools and seatbelted bash only the current Space's roots, and removes access on archive. Focused service/UI/seatbelt tests passed 14/14 (including a real macOS read-allow/write-deny run); route tests passed 3/3. Typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, diff and bundle budgets passed. Shell manifest `aae78ffd`; serial `/tmp/aux-s12-workspaces-ax-native-build.log` passed 21 steps and the runtime was staged. Disposable [`--workspaces-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-workspaces-ui-2026-09-28-b/manifest.json) passed review, read-only grant and archive through native Settings. Its first attempt exposed a Chromium accessibility DCHECK for an unlabeled generic review container; the final build uses a named group. A native model run reading the folder was not exercised; the focused seatbelt test covers file confinement. Continue the remaining Workspaces escalation semantics and other roadmap dependencies.

**September 28, 2026 Hooks checkpoint.** Tasks now registers a run-end Hook only for an existing standing Space MCP grant. It shows the exact handler fingerprint and arguments for approval, offers a supervised test using the same executor, records invocations, and revokes future calls. The runtime deduplicates terminal run events and marks interrupted calls uncertain. Focused Hook/MCP/UI tests passed 12/12 after dist rebuild; typecheck, lint, format, generated bindings, bundle budgets and diff checks passed. Shell manifest `c19b886a`, serial `/tmp/aux-s12-hooks-test-native-build.log` (21 steps), and runtime staging passed. Disposable [`--hooks-ui`](../release/test-artifacts/native-s12-hooks-ui-2026-09-28-c/manifest.json) passed native approval, exact tool call, completed invocation and revocation. Automatic run-end dispatch has runtime test coverage but no separate native model run. Other Hook events and command/prompt/agent handlers remain unavailable. Continue S12 Workspaces and deeper Goal rules.

## Current goal and boundary

Deliver a **working native Chromium development build** with the roadmap's product features and native integration, preserving Aux branding, liquid glass, and both horizontal and vertical Aside layouts. Implement and test migration with fixtures; **do not migrate the owner's real data**. Final DMG, release signing/notarization, full release qualification, and the takeover package are deferred. The current build is a development checkpoint, **not** a completed product handoff or release.

Build and test locally on this Mac. Do not enable paid GitHub Actions billing. Tell the owner before using a Linux VM. No Linux VM or paid Actions run was used for this checkpoint. Keep AI Gateway fixture-only, on `meta/muse-spark-1.3-contributor` and within the $50 cumulative cap; reconcile the spend ledger before another model run.

## Exact working state

- Repository: `robzilla1738/vector`, private; branch `codex/aux-roadmap-build`. PR [#24](https://github.com/robzilla1738/vector/pull/24) is open against `main`. Local `main` is not this branch. A push denial or a missing Actions check is not a green CI claim. Do not enable paid Actions.
- Native source: Chromium `154.0.8037.58` in `/Users/rcourson/aux-chromium-stable/chromium/src`, with ordered Aux patches **0001–0095** from [patches/series](../chromium/patches/series). This checkout is intentionally modified by those patches. Patch 0053 prepares site-permission listing/reset, filtered website-data clearing, and an Aux HTTPS-upgrade preference; patch 0054 prepares a Profile tab-audio snapshot; patch 0055 prepares validated external-link handoff; patch 0056 prepares conservative frame-level unsaved-work detection; patch 0057 prepares file opening and Finder reveal, with `openPath` additionally refusing executable files and paths inside launchable bundles. Patches 0053–0057 compiled in AuxDev. Their focused site controls, tab audio, unsaved-work, and file handoff journeys passed with disposable Profiles; the external-link handoff has native build evidence but no dedicated live journey. Clean full-series replay is recorded through 0036; later patches have incremental application/build evidence, not a fresh full replay claim. Patch 0048 stays the capability manifest. Patch 0049 enables WebMCP for non-private profiles. Patch 0050 is the background paint lease. Patch 0051 publishes shell events. Patch 0052 adds download services. Patch 0058 routes main-frame HTTP authentication through Aux’s shell prompt. Patch 0059 supplies window-scoped Chromium tab thumbnails to the shell. Patch 0060 opens Chromium’s native back/forward history menu for the active tab. Patch 0061 adds the native shell context menu. Patch 0062 connects Reader to Chromium’s DOM distiller. Patch 0063 replaces four Webium window stubs with native platform handlers. Patch 0064 adds native save, plugin directory, and bounded evidence file dialogs. Patch 0065 adds workbook and stored-artifact exports through the native save panel. Patch 0066 adds scoped external agent connection creation, listing, client detection, and revocation; the pinned runtime package now includes its MCP entry point. Patch 0067 qualifies `inboundAgents` in Chromium’s embedded host after a packaged MCP read and post-revocation denial. Patch 0068 adds page-level Ask and Summarize actions to the native shell context menu and publishes `onPageAction` to the owning window.
- Privacy & Security: patch 0089 and shell manifest `6648059c` compiled in `/tmp/aux-s9-https-first-native-build.log`. The disposable `--settings-https-first` journey passed at `release/test-artifacts/native-s9-https-first-mode-diagnostic-2026-09-27` for Aux's optional silent HTTPS upgrade control: switch off produced HTTP and on produced a TLS ClientHello after Chromium's independent HTTPS-First heuristics were disabled in the fixture. The unmodified default heuristic path can still attempt HTTPS when the Aux switch is off. Do not claim this control overrides Chromium HTTPS-Only, balanced, or policy modes; their interaction and Private behavior remain to qualify. Next Privacy dependencies: clear-data counts, content-blocking lists, cookies, and consent controls.
- Content blocking: patch 0090 proxies renderer-created subresources through the browser process and checks each redirect target against the current Profile rules. The serial `/tmp/aux-s9-blocking-redirect-guard-build-2.log` succeeded; patch reverse-check and native validation passed. The disposable `--settings-content-blocking` journey at `release/test-artifacts/native-s9-content-blocking-redirect-guard-2026-09-27` passed built-in tracker cancellation, off/on restoration, and redirect cancellation with a loopback network oracle and CUA toggle state. `--settings-custom-list` at `release/test-artifacts/native-s9-custom-list-2026-09-27` passed a pinned nine-domain HTTPS list download, native snapshot install, cancellation of a domain absent from the built-in seed, and restoration when the list was paused. `feature:contentBlocking` is qualified for source `970adc61`; the final shell manifest is `5b9a112c` (433 KB startup JS, 178 KB CSS, 1,438 KB total JS). The earlier failed subresource and redirect runs are diagnostics. Custom-list refresh, cookie-banner stylesheet behavior, site exceptions, and Private-mode behavior still need focused live verification; continue Privacy & Security counts, cookies, consent, and full Settings document reload.
- Profiles: patch 0069 compiles seven native `ProfileManager` methods. The manager now has its own `/settings/profiles` route and is visible only when the native `profiles` stage is `on`. The `feature:profiles` qualification is recorded at source `970adc61`; the serial `/tmp/aux-s9-profiles-qualification-build.log` and disposable `--profiles` journey at `release/test-artifacts/native-s9-profiles-qualified-2-2026-09-27` passed create/open, rename, archive, restore, explicit confirmed deletion and directory cleanup after restart. The owner approved deletion of this named disposable fixture at action time. Open-in-Profile URL remains unverified. An earlier wrong-window attempt created an empty `Roadmap Disposable` Profile in the default Aux data directory; it remains for explicit cleanup. No pre-existing Profile was deleted.
- Native window polish: patch 0070 removes the hard-coded macOS glass radius, uses `NSWindow.isOnActiveSpace`, and persists theme/glass/material in Chromium Profile prefs for startup. AuxDev built, patch reverse-check passed, and the disposable `--appearance-prefs` journey at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T03-50-54-242Z` verified Light/Title bar values across restart. The final build `/tmp/aux-dev-window-polish-final-build.log` and focused `--edge-leeway` journey at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T04-14-25-776Z` verified floating Aside entry, 8 px exit leeway, and dismissal over an embedded page. The native edge watcher samples the active window cursor because guest mouse moves do not reach its AppKit event observer. First-frame pixels and cross-Space behavior remain unverified.
- Navigation recovery: patch 0071 compiled and passed four disposable local journeys. It publishes current main-frame failures to the catalog and runtime, and accepts certificate or HTTP fallback only for the owning window’s matching interstitial. The default Chromium 154 HTTPS warning dialog remains the default UI; Aux’s HTTP control was verified with that newer dialog feature disabled. Cross-window and stale-error rejection remain to verify.
- Private health and diagnostics: patch 0072 adds Profile-local aggregate browser-process CPU/memory, UI scheduling delay, window/tab counts, page crash/hang counts, a Clear action, and a native support-report save dialog. The disposable `--health-diagnostics` journey passed at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T05-05-01-884Z`: it checked the stored sample, Settings UI, saved JSON privacy fields, and Clear. The final journey checked that Clear retains the current session counter, and the final build and patch check are recorded in [EVIDENCE.md](EVIDENCE.md). Startup and suspension telemetry, runtime version in the report, long-span retention, and crash/hang injection still need coverage. Bridge coverage is 19 missing renderer-used members.
- Unpacked extensions: patch 0073 adds native folder selection and update of the installed source folder. The disposable `--unpacked-extension` journey passed at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T05-27-25-991Z`: Settings showed version 1.0.0 after installation and 1.0.1 after Update with the same Chromium extension ID. `feature:extensions` is qualified for the pinned source identity. The first live attempt found a UI-thread blocking I/O crash; the final build removed that check and the journey passed. Bridge coverage is 17 missing renderer-used members. Store installation and permission-heavy unpacked extensions still need focused journeys.
- Runtime backup and restore: patch 0074 registers five native shell methods for local backup creation, folder-picked restore staging, pending status and cancellation, and layout export. The runtime now validates staged database/artifact digests before startup, refuses a live peer or changed artifact, preserves a private SQLite recovery snapshot, and swaps the database only after checks pass. The disposable `--runtime-backup-restore` journey passed at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T05-47-56-592Z`: a post-backup workspace disappeared after restart and remained in the recovery snapshot. The focused runtime tests cover successful artifact restore, tampering, conflict, live peer, and interrupted-swap recovery. `feature:electronMigration` is qualified for the pinned source identity; cancel, layout export, large backup timeouts, and failure injection still need live coverage. Bridge coverage is 12 missing renderer-used members.
- Development app: `/Users/rcourson/aux-chromium-stable/chromium/src/out/AuxDev/Aux.app`. The latest serial native build includes patch 0090 and shell manifest `5b9a112ca50730f3d0ee721eef8a7fd3983fe3535b6cd70f5a97540c4f3db571` (`/tmp/aux-s9-blocking-redirect-guard-build-2.log`), followed by runtime staging. Generated host methods are 156 methods and 19 events at hash `3c442dad0021`, staged against source identity `970adc61`; ten feature qualifications now match that identity and their scoped evidence. The separate official static build remains deferred with its cache preserved.
- Native journeys that passed: six core runtime RPC journeys; two disposable Profile restart/isolation fixture journeys; Aux Home, live page navigation, native Find and page zoom; print preview and DevTools; Open File; PDF save/cancel; page-focused default shortcuts; a user-rebound Command Palette shortcut, subsequently reset. These are scoped results, not full feature coverage.
- Visual state: the owner confirmed the corrected right-only assistant corners. Docked glass uses the common native window material/tint; narrow floating left and right panels now share the same tint and blur tokens. Perceptual blur across displays, widths and Reduce Transparency still needs live inspection. Screenshots alone do not establish native glass quality.
- Reader: the guided `--reader` journey passed on a disposable Profile at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T01-50-17-993Z`; it showed distilled prose, a heading and a safe relative link while preserving the original page. The native request is page/navigation-scoped and bounded; the shell parses via a named Trusted Types policy into a restricted node tree. The focused sanitizer test passed. This does not qualify arbitrary pages, offline copies, or cancellation behavior. The generated host feature stage was staged, then its header source identity restored to `e5ffa8a7` to match the ordered patch stack before the final native build.
- Webium window services: patch 0063 compiles (`/tmp/aux-dev-window-platform-build.log`) and implements the pending-download close dialog callback, workspace reporting, emoji panel, and macOS eyedropper. Its patch reverse/forward replay matches the native file byte-for-byte. The guided `--eyedropper` fixture reached `EyeDropper.open()` on a disposable Profile, but the macOS sampler did not return a selected color through computer input; `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T01-58-34-354Z` is a failed journey. These services are not live-qualified.
- Native file I/O: patch 0064 compiles (`/tmp/aux-dev-file-io-build.log`), and its isolated reverse/forward replay is byte-identical across four files. The disposable `--file-io` journey passed at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T02-12-32-437Z`: Library exported a bookmark through the macOS save panel to the selected path, and the harness checked the title and URL in the HTML. Plugin folder and evidence image/document selections compile but still need their own focused live journeys. Bridge coverage is now 24 missing renderer-used members after patch 0069. The first export attempt timed out while reaching Library; its failed artifact remains local.
- Document artifact export: patch 0065 compiles (`/tmp/aux-dev-document-export-build.log`), and isolated reverse/forward replay is byte-identical across four native files. The host only runtime service renders bounded XLSX values and checks stored artifact paths remain inside Aux storage; the native picker copies artifacts to user-selected destinations. Focused disposable `--workbook-export` and `--artifact-export` journeys passed at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T02-27-49-984Z` and `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T02-30-27-739Z`. Both used the actual `chrome://aux/` host method through Chromium’s local inspector, with no public debugging port or model call. The workbook was parsed and its formula-looking value remained text; the artifact export preserved PNG bytes. These qualify the native paths, not the surrounding result-preview UI.
- Native page actions: patch 0068 built (`/tmp/aux-dev-page-context-actions-build.log`) and its isolated replay is byte-identical across the two native files. The disposable `--page-action` journey passed at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T03-09-00-689Z`: the native context menu’s Summarize page action opened a page-scoped draft with the current loopback URL. Ask and selection-specific actions and cross-window behavior remain unverified. Bridge coverage is 31 missing renderer-used members.
- External agents: patch 0066 compiles (`/tmp/aux-dev-agent-connections-build.log`) and its isolated reverse/forward replay is byte-identical across two native files. Patch 0067 compiles (`/tmp/aux-dev-inbound-agents-qualification-build.log`) and activates `inboundAgents` in the embedded host for source identity `e5ffa8a7`. The disposable `--agent-connections` journey passed after activation at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T02-57-11-198Z`: the packaged MCP client read only the scoped workspace and was denied after native revocation; the token file and Profile were removed. Settings showed detected Codex and Claude Code clients and enabled workspace connection creation. Bridge coverage is 32 missing renderer-used members.
- Known unfinished integration: native Profiles management is still feature-gated; Quick Ask is active for the pinned source identity but its physical global hotkey has not been observed; many bridge methods, agent/product surfaces, extension/passkey/import flows, both-layout accessibility and complete visual journeys remain. Do not switch their gates on to conceal missing native services. The earlier Profiles experiment was removed before staging.
- Credential handoff: ordered patch 0075 compiles Chromium's save/update offer producer and one-use, page/origin-scoped save/dismiss handlers. The shell receives an owning-window offer without the password and forwards its `offerId`; navigation clears old tokens. A disposable loopback form produced the visible Aux Save prompt after one submission, and a focused rerun verified zero saved rows before consent. Chromium's native bubble did not appear when the Aux path was gated, because WebUI Browser has no toolbar provider; the minimal password prompt is now core, while the broader Passwords and Autofill feature remains preview. The Save action awaited computer-use confirmation and timed out. Persistence, dismiss, update, stale-offer, and cross-window checks remain open. See [EVIDENCE.md](EVIDENCE.md).
- Link hover: patch 0076 replaces WebUI Browser's empty `GetStatusBubbles` result with an Aux status producer. Chromium's shared delegate had assumed every bubble was a Views object; the first disposable run crashed and the corrected route now handles WebUI without that cast. The final build passed at `/tmp/aux-dev-link-status-final-build.log`. The focused `--link-status` journey at `release/test-artifacts/native-journeys-2026-09-27T07-12-29-085Z` displayed a loopback link's destination on hover and cleared it when the pointer left. The owning-window filter is compiled but not separately exercised across two windows.

1. Landed, and not to be redone: S6.7 external sessions are runs (`e16672f4`). S3 fixture submit confirmation passed (`posts: 1`). S4 WebMCP passed (`webmcpWrites: 1`). The actor actuation spike was measured and rejected (`986381b4`); the probe is not in the patch series. The paint lease passed with 352 background frames in 2.5s (`531d3549`). S7 shell events passed one loading journey (`sawLoading: true`, `c6d526c2`). Details and manifests are in [EVIDENCE.md](EVIDENCE.md).
   The offline S4 observation fixture was rechecked at 2,806/850/1,562 characters with all six facts found; [the report](evidence/observation-fixtures-2026-09-26.json) records the rows. Its two-step form fixture now measures revision-matched deltas 88.6% and 86.9% smaller than full compact snapshots, passing the ≥40% gate. The real-Chromium `driver-world` integration suite passes 11/11, including select, check, upload, alert, open shadow DOM with ID-free sibling hosts, punctuated IDs and quoted test IDs, and a separately targeted cross-site iframe subtree fill/click. Native AnnotatedPageContent comparison is still missing. The runtime's delayed observation maintenance now drains before repository close.
2. S5 is not done. The loop at `bfc70693` has pass@1 95.3%, mean calls 2.640, and multi-step mean 4.246. The planner baseline remains 46% / 1.707 / 2.362. The deletion bar needs multi-step mean calls at or under about 1.653. A reproduced `L12` intake failure came from a generated CSS path for the Email checkbox ref that matched two nested labels; direct-child path generation now makes the real fixture `check r7` complete, with 11/11 driver integration tests including escaped punctuation IDs and quoted test ID fallback. The owner supplied a new fixture-only Gateway credential. The inherited shell key conflicted with the ignored `.env` key and caused two invalid zero-call attempts; the benchmark harness now rejects that mismatch before any model call. With the inherited value removed, one scoped `L12` Muse Spark 1.3 Contributor trial passed its independent form-submission oracle in two model calls and no duplicate writes. The later 50×3 A/B run was interrupted during loop trial 3 when the records fixture stopped accepting connections. No complete report or S5 deletion decision exists. The harness now writes partial reports atomically after each row and marks fixture oracle outages invalid; offline validation passes. Do not repeat the full benchmark without a specific reason or reliability change. The shared ledger after the interrupted run showed $2.513041753 committed, $0.006025001 reserved, and $47.480933246 remaining; reconcile again before any model run. Gateway caching stays auto. Direct Anthropic cache breakpoints and the OpenAI session cache key are already in the loop client and unit-tested. The planner modules stay until the full gate passes.
3. S7 is not done. `node scripts/check-bridge-coverage.mjs --check` now reports zero renderer-used members without a native handler or event producer after built patch 0081; the 0052 registry snapshot reported 57 after correcting the script to count the built-in `capabilities` dispatcher. The script exits 0 unless `--check` is passed. Native downloads, site controls, tab audio, unsaved work, file handoff, Reader, page actions, link hover status, and the core password prompt have compiled patches and focused evidence in [EVIDENCE.md](EVIDENCE.md); bridge coverage only counts handlers. Patch 0077 adds the shell route to Chromium's guarded password fill; its disposable HTTP refusal journey passed, while successful HTTPS fill remains unverified. Patch 0078 connects Fill personal info to a Chromium address profile; the disposable migration and live loopback form-fill journey passed. Patch 0079 adds encrypted Chromium Profile password import/export and the Password Manager Settings route. Its wrong-passphrase refusal was visible in a disposable live UI, and the closed Profile had zero fixture password rows. Export now requires fresh OS device authentication. Positive import/export still needs the action-time confirmed synthetic credential journey; the automated helper did not observe the visible error text, so no passing transfer harness result is claimed. Ordinary HTTP refusal and card clearing still need focused live verification. `openExternal` still needs a dedicated live handoff journey. Continue S7 focused verification of the compiled services; zero bridge gaps alone does not qualify the remaining behavior. `onVaultOffer` is produced and window-scoped, but password persistence is unverified pending an approved save journey. `onTranslationChanged` now has a native producer and a passing loopback translation/restore journey; `onPageAction` has native page-level menu actions, while selection actions remain open. Quick Ask opens in a compact native WebUI window and carries an unsent draft into a full-size window; its global shortcut registered, but synthetic key events did not verify invocation. Profiles has seven native handlers and a passing disposable create/rename/archive/restore/delete/restart-cleanup journey; its feature stage is qualified at source 970adc61. Continue the remaining S7 behavioral checks and advance the independent S8 design-system dependency in substantial batches, then S9 through S14.
   The full repository unit suite passed 258 files / 1,321 tests before the latest HTTP-auth shell response change; its focused new test passes 1/1, and typecheck, changed-file lint, format, and renderer bundle budgets pass. A `false` response from `respondHttpAuth` now reports failure and re-enables the form. Patch 0058 registers the HTTP-auth methods, exposes a unique pending main-frame challenge from Chromium’s `LoginTabHelper`, and routes responses to the current window without presenting the native dialog in parallel. The guided `--http-auth` loopback journey passed: the Aux prompt appeared, the protected page stayed closed until fixture credentials were submitted, then loaded and dismissed the prompt. Wrong credentials and successful retry passed a second focused live run; cancellation also passed a focused live journey; cross-window rejection still needs coverage. Patch 0059 adds native tab previews; the disposable `--preview` journey passed with a background tab image. Patch 0060 adds the native back-history menu; its keyboard journey passed, while pointer opening remains unverified. Patch 0061 adds the shell context menu; its Shift-F10 Reload journey passed. Reader and scoped navigation recovery are verified; continue the remaining bridge services. The current bridge coverage check is zero, but the named live verification gaps remain. Patch 0071 has local failure, certificate, Aux HTTP fallback, and default Chromium warning journeys recorded in the evidence ledger.
4. Build and verify with the [runbook](../experiments/chromium-fork/CONTINUATION.md). Build each coherent native batch when ready, with one native build at a time. Run focused live journeys and relevant safety checks once for the batch; repeat tests or benchmarks only to resolve a specific risk. Quit Aux before relinking. After `apps/runtime/src` edits, run `npx tsc -b` before unit tests that import `@aux/runtime`. The observation-maintenance and detached-page list fixes are rebuilt and staged into AuxDev. Shell tests resolve contracts from source. Do not run `prettier --write` on broad directories. Record each slice in [EVIDENCE.md](EVIDENCE.md). Keep migration on disposable fixtures. Never use the owner's real Profile.
5. Follow the v2 dependency order. The native host's method and capability manifest must precede Electron removal and feature activation. Keep Profile/window identity and trusted WebUI authorization in the browser process; verify affected features through fixture journeys in both Aside layouts.
6. Keep branch `codex/aux-roadmap-build` and PR #24 in sync after reviewed work. Do not enable paid Actions billing or claim a green check from an older SHA. A normal protected-main merge still requires its `CI passed` check.
